Let's think about coordinates and exact local refinement.

Given a normalized sequence p, if we know objective value O and max coefficient locations K. To reduce max, we can shift mass from variables contributing to max coefficients. For active coefficients k, q_k=∑_{i} p_i p_{k-i}. If coefficient k active, pairs i,k-i contribute. To reduce q_k without changing sum, we can decrease p_i or p_j, increase elsewhere. But decreasing a p_i affects many q. Coordinate descent with line search might be effective.

Simple local update: Choose a mass transfer from a to b: p'_a=p_a - δ, p'_b=p_b + δ. Keep nonnegative. The objective as function of δ is piecewise smooth. We can find δ that minimizes local max among affected coefficients. n large but choose a,b random or those with high gradient. Could be slow.

Maybe use "exchange" of two coordinate blocks: shift a window of mass. Since n thousands, block moves may improve.

Previous algorithm uses global gradient and restarts. We can improve by using second-order (L-BFGS) in log-space to converge faster to local optimum. Let's design hybrid:
1. Initialize f from parent, maybe also generate several candidates.
2. Run a robust smoothed max descent with line searches: iterate
   - compute q = conv f (FFT), current max M.
   - choose weights w = softmax((q - M)/τ) or all active equal? Use adaptive τ.
   - compute gradient g = 2 * (w * f) (cyclic trick).
   - Project gradient to simplex tangent: d = g - f*(sum f*g?)? Because log-space uses f*(grad - mean). For normalized p, restricting log weights tangent means v = grad - f∑grad? Actually if optimizing in log weights w_i with softmax p, gradient in log w is p_i * grad_i (if removing constant? Let's derive. Let p=softmax(w), ∂p_i/∂w_j = p_i(δ_{ij}-p_j). Implement Adam+backtrack:
```
def log_adam_step(w, grad, state, lr):
  m = β1 m + (1-β1) grad
  v = β2 v + (1-β2) grad^2
  dw = m/(sqrt(v)+eps)
  dw = clip(dw,-maxdw,maxdw)
  w_new = w - lr*dw
  # renormalize? w subtract max
```
In run, maintain m,v,t. When p changes drastically, reset state.

Implement direct step with backtracking:
```
def try_self_step(f, A, q, w, w_state, lr):
  # w = weights from q
  h = reversed_weighted(f,w) # uses RDF_f
  g = 2*h
  grad_log = f*(g - dot(f,g))
  wnew, ... = adam
  fnew = softmax(wnew)
  score = fft_score(fnew)
  if score < f_score: accept
  else retry smaller lr (0.5)
```

def active_weights(c, beta=None, desired=None):
  m=max(c)
  if beta is None: choose based on sorted? Simpler: if m<=0 return uniform.
  tol = 1e-12 + 1e-8*m
  act = c >= m - tol
  if act.sum() < min_active or act.sum()>max: beta = ...
  x=(c-m)/m
  y=clip(beta*x, -700,0)
  w=exp(y); w/=sum

Search runner details:
We can create a single `optimize_sequence(a_start, target_n, seed, budget, verbose)` that:

Main loop more concretely:
```
def optimize_sequence(start, target_n, seed, budget):
  a = upsample(start,target_n)
  n=a.size
  N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
  phase=...
  f=normalize(a)
  A, q, score = fft_score_A_c(f,N)
  best_f=f.copy(); best_score=score; top=[]
  b=f.copy(); w_b=log; m_b,v_b,t_b=zeros
  F=np.fft.rfft(f,N); RDF=phase*conj(F)
  w_state_f zeros for self
  beta_h=8; beta_s=8; lr_h=0.08; lr_s=0.05
  stall=0; outer=0
  while time<deadline:
    # update F/RDF each outer based on f? yes if f changed
    # inner: run inner_steps maybe adapt.
    max_inner = 8 + (outer//100 capped 32)
    improved_outer_no
    for s in range(max_inner):
       B=rfft(b,N)
       h=irfft(F*B,N)[:L]; clip
       H=max(h)
       # if H close previous and previous no improvement maybe break
       w=make_weights(h,beta_h)
       Pw=rfft(w,N)
       hgrad=irfft(Pw*RDF,N)[n-1:n+n-1] # length n
       grad_log=b*(hgrad - dot(b,hgrad))
       b_new,w_b_new,... = adam(grad_log,b,w_b,m_b,v_b,t_b,lr_h)
       # quick score? The inner objective H after update requires another B and h; can compute next loop. To accept, if H_new not too worse, accept else rollback and reduce lr.
       # We can just accept Adam; if finite.
       b=b_new
    # after inner, compute H (maybe from last) and update f candidates
    # maybe compute F score if f changed: A,q,score...
    # t line search:
    local_best_t=0; local_best_score=f_score
    for t in [0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0]:
       cand=(1-t)f + t b
       normalize; sc=fft_score(cand)
       if sc < local_best_score: ...
    if best_t>0 and local_best_score < f_score:
       f=cand; f_score local_best
       update best; reset self state; update F/RDF; if best_t==1: b=f.copy? else keep b
       stall=0; lr_h maybe increase
    else:
       stall++; lr_h decrease
    # periodic self gradient (e.g., every 8 outer or if stall big)
    if outer%8==0:
       # compute A,q,score for f (if not recently)
       # try up to 5 self Adam steps with backtracking
    # restart if stall >...
```

def fourier_perturb(base, K, sigma, rng):
   w = log(base)
   # add random smooth: for freq 1..K, amplitude sigma/random, random phase
   x = np.arange(n)
   delta = 0
   for k in range(1,K+1):
      amp = sigma / k**0.7?
      ph = rng.uniform(0,2π)
      delta += amp * np.cos(2π k x/n + ph)
   return softmax(w+delta)
```

  if m<=1: return 1

  while p<m: p<<=1
  return p

   if m<=0: uniform
   x=(v-m)/m
   # If beta very high, clip -700
   y=beta*x
   y=np.clip(y,-700,0)
   e=np.exp(y)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: one hot at argmax

Adam state update:
```
def adam_state_get(size): return np.zeros(size), np.zeros(size), 0

def adam_step(w, grad, m, v, t, lr, max_step=1.0):
  beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999
  t+=1
  m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*grad
  v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*grad*grad
  mhat = m/(1-beta1**t)
  vhat = v/(1-beta2**t)
  dw = mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+1e-12)
  dw = np.where(np.isfinite(dw),dw,0)
  dw = np.clip(dw,-max_step,max_step)
  w_new = w - lr*dw
  w_new = np.where(np.isfinite(w_new),w_new,0)
  w_new -= max(w_new)
  return w_new,m,v,t
```
But if grad all zeros, v stays zero, dw zero. Good. M/V accumulation in place modifies arrays; if we need previous for rollback, copy? We can update and if rollback, need old w and old states. To simplify, no rollback for inner. For self line search, we can compute candidate w and score; if accept, update state; if not, keep old. Need state copy? We can compute m_new,v_new without modifying, then if not accept, discard. Implement function returns new arrays? Copy cost O(n) per self step; okay. For inner many steps, modify in place no rollback. For self maybe use in-place but if not accepted, revert w state copy? We'll create copies: `m_new = m*...; v_new=...`; if accept assign. Since n=32768, copy 6 arrays maybe 0.2MB, fine.

But for inner, if we modify m/v and b becomes invalid, no rollback. Accept if finite. Good.

Let's outline optimize_sequence code in detail:
```
def optimize_sequence(a_start, target_n, seed, budget, verbose=True):
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+max(0,budget-2)
    f = resize_sequence(a_start, target_n)
    n=f.size; N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    L=2*n-1
    phase = np.exp(-2j*np.pi*np.arange(N//2+1)*(n-1)/float(N))
    A, q, f_score = fft_score_A_c(f,N,L)
    best_f=f.copy(); best_score=f_score; top=[(best_score,best_f)]
    log_b = log_weights_from_sequence(f); b=f.copy()
    F = np.fft.rfft(f,N); RDF = phase*conj(F)
    log_f = log_weights_from_sequence(f)
    m_b=np.zeros(n); v_b=np.zeros(n); t_b=0
    m_f=np.zeros(n); v_f=np.zeros(n); t_f=0
    beta_h=5.0; beta_s=8.0; lr_h=0.08; lr_s=0.05
    stall=0; outer=0; last_self=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # initial random perturb if uniform: if f_score >1.9 try random
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       outer+=1
       # sanity
       if not finite: reset
       # ensure F/RDF match f (maybe after direct self)
       # inner steps
       inner_steps = 8
       if outer%5==0: inner_steps +=4
       if stall>10: inner_steps += min(32, stall*2)
       inner_steps = min(inner_steps, 60)
       H = None
       for s in range(inner_steps):
           B = np.fft.rfft(b,N)
           hraw = np.fft.irfft(F*B,N)[:L]
           hraw = np.maximum(hraw,0.0)
           H = float(np.max(hraw)) if hraw.size else 0.0
           if not finite H: reset b=f break
           w = make_weights(hraw,beta_h)
           W = np.fft.rfft(w,N)
           g = np.fft.irfft(W*RDF,N)
           seg = g[n-1:n+n-1]
           seg = np.maximum(seg,0.0)
           grad_log = b*(seg - np.dot(b,seg))
           # if grad norm zero break
           if np.max(np.abs(grad_log)) < 1e-15: break
           log_b, m_b, v_b, t_b = adam_step(log_b, grad_log, m_b, v_b, t_b, lr_h)
           b = softmax(log_b)
       # line search toward b
       if all finite and b finite:
          # maybe ensure b sum1
          best_t=0; best_cand_score=f_score
          for t in (0.5,0.25,0.75,1.0,0.125,0.875):
             cand=f*(1-t)+b*t
             if cand max<=0: continue
             cand/=cand.sum()
             sc=fft_score(cand,N)
             if sc < best_cand_score - 1e-14:
                 best_cand_score=sc; best_cand=cand; best_t=t
          if best_t>0 and best_cand_score < f_score:
              f=best_cand; f_score=best_cand_score
              log_f=log_weights(f); reset m_f,v_f,t_f
              F=rfft(f); RDF=phase*conj(F)
              if best_t>=0.999: b=f.copy(); log_b=log_weights(b); reset b state
              else: # keep b but maybe normalize
                  if not finite b: reset
              if f_score <= best_score+1e-13:
                  if f_score < best_score: update best/top
                  stall=0; lr_h=min(0.2, lr_h*1.02)
              else: (shouldn't)
              last_self=outer? maybe no
          else:
              stall += 1
              lr_h=max(0.02, lr_h*0.95)
              # if no improve and b far: gradually pull b to f in log
              # d_log = log_b - mean? not mean; log_b_center=log_b - max; log_f_center... pull 0.1
              if stall>5:
                  log_b = 0.9*log_b + 0.1*log_f; b=softmax(log_b); reset b state? maybe not
       else:
          stall+=1; reset b=f
       # beta schedule
       if outer%20==0:
          beta_h = min(60.0, beta_h*1.05+0.2)
          beta_s = min(160.0, beta_s*1.05+0.2)
          lr_h = max(0.03, 0.08/(1+beta_h/50.0))
          lr_s = max(0.02, 0.05/(1+beta_s/100.0))
       # self step
       if (outer - last_self >= 15 or stall>25) and time.monotonic() < deadline:
           last_self=outer
           # compute current q if not recent? We may not have q since f update.
           A,q,sq = fft_score_A_c(f,N,L)
           if sq < f_score: f_score=sq; update best
           improved_any=False
           # try up to 5 self steps
           for _ in range(5):
               w = make_weights(q,beta_s)
               W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
               g=np.fft.irfft(W*RDF,N)[n-1:n+n-1]
               g=np.maximum(g,0)
               grad_log=f*(2*g - 2*np.dot(f,g))  # factor 2
               # candidate using m_f (copy)
               log_f_try,m_f_try,v_f_try,t_f_try = adam_step(log_f, grad_log, m_f, v_f, t_f, lr_s, copy=True)
               cand=softmax(log_f_try)
               sc=fft_score(cand,N)
               # Maybe if sc not improve, try half lr
               if sc < f_score - 1e-14:
                   f=cand; f_score=sc; q? Need recompute q for next step
                   log_f=m...; update F/RDF; reset b=f
                   A,q,_ = fft_score_A_c(f,N,L)
                   if f_score < best_score: update best/top
                   improved_any=True
                   break  # one self step per schedule
               else:
                   lr_s = max(0.01, lr_s*0.7)
           if not improved_any:
               stall += 5? maybe
       # restart
       if stall > 60:
           if f_score > best_score + 0.02 or rng.random()<0.5:
               f=best_f.copy(); f_score=best_score
           else:
               # Fourier perturb best_f
               sig=rng.choice([0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2])
               K=max(2,n//256)
               w_try=fourier_log_perturb(best_f,K,sig,rng)
               f=softmax(w_try); f_score=fft_score(f,N)
               if not finite or f_score > best_score*1.05: f=best_f...
           log_f=...; F/RDF; b=f; reset states; stall=0; lr_h=0.08; lr_s=0.05
    return f,best_f,best_score,top
```

Fourier perturb:
```
def fourier_log_perturb(a, K, sigma, rng):
   n=a.size
   w=log_weights(a)
   if K<1: return w
   x=np.arange(n)
   delta=np.zeros(n)
   for k in range(1,K+1):
      amp = sigma / (k**0.5)
      ph = rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi)
      delta += amp * np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x/n + ph)
   # maybe low-pass filter to reduce high freq? K small.
   w = w + delta
   w -= max(w)
   return w
```
Sigma in log units. For n=32768, K maybe 8 to 32. Could be too much; choose sig 0.02-0.1.

Maybe add random small full Gaussian at restart too. Use Fourier for smooth.

`adam_inplace` details:
```
def adam_inplace(w, grad, m, v, t, lr, max_step=1.0):
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999
    t += 1
    m *= beta1; m += (1-beta1)*grad
    v *= beta2; v += (1-beta2)*(grad*grad)
    mhat = m/(1-beta1**t)
    vhat = v/(1-beta2**t)
    dw = mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-12)
    dw = np.where(np.isfinite(dw), dw, 0.0)
    dw = np.clip(dw, -max_step, max_step)
    w = w - lr*dw
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(w))
    if np.isfinite(mx): w -= mx
    return w, t
```
But `grad*grad` allocates. Could do `g2=grad*grad`; okay. If grad very large, v large. dw finite.

What about using a known recursive construction to improve constant: Take p_n as the first n coefficients of a positive polynomial R whose square is as flat as possible. We can solve for p via iterative deconvolution targeting uniform q. Specifically, we can run a fixed-point algorithm:
Given current p, q=p*p. Define target y = q with max clipped to current max? To design p with flatter q, set y_k = min(q_k, M_target) maybe, and compute p_new as nonnegative square root of y (formal recurrence from first n coefficients) and normalize. Choose M_target decreasing. This is like "iterative square root clipping". If we set target y uniform T, recurrence produces sqrt flat. To improve, we can alternate:
- Given p, compute q.
- Choose T = max(q) * factor (<1).
- Define target coefficients z_k = min(q_k, T) maybe (clip high) for all k? Need p_new such that p_new*p_new≈z. Compute z length 2n-1 (clipped q), then solve for p_new by recurrence from first n coefficients z_0..z_{n-1} (ensuring nonnegative via projection) and maybe project to nonnegative. This directly reduces max at the cost of p positivity.
This is a different algorithm! Let's examine.

Formal square root recurrence: Given target first n coefficients z_0..z_{n-1}, produce p with p0=sqrt(z0) and p_k=(z_k - S_k)/(2p0). Need z_k ≥ S_k. If not, set p_k=0 and adjust? We can project z to feasible: largest possible p_k given zmax? To maximize p_k, set z_k as high as T; but if T small? Since we want lower max, T = current max maybe. Feasibility if z_k ≥ S_k. If some z_k<S_k due clipping, p_k would negative. We can set p_k=0 for negative and then update? This is like iterative thresholding on polynomial square root. It might converge to a sequence with flat square coefficients and nonnegative.

Could be powerful and cheap O(n^2) recurrence? Recurrence naively S_k is convolution of p with itself first k, could do O(n^2) if direct; for n=32768 impossible. But can compute p_new from z via FFT? We need solve triangular square root; can use Newton iteration in convolution algebra? Given z, find p such that p^2≈z in first terms. Use polynomial square root via FFT? There are algorithms O(n log n). But simpler recurrence O(n^2) too slow for high n. However for low-res coarse n=2048 maybe. Could use iterative Newton in Fourier: p_new = 0.5(p + z * inv?) not for truncated. Maybe not.

But we can implement recurrence in O(n^2) for n=1024/2048 only as coarse construction. It might produce better low-res shaped sequence.

Alternative: Since exact convolution O(n^2), local optimization at n=4096 already O(n log n).

Maybe use `p = sqrt(q)` in the group algebra via Fourier with cyclic length L=2n-1. If q is target, compute P = ifft(sqrt(fft(q)))? Because cyclic convolution square equals q if DFT squared = Q. If q nonnegative real, Q can be complex; sqrt of Q complex; IDFT may not be real/nonnegative. But we can take real part/positive. This is a global operation O(n log n). Maybe good as a construction step:
- p current, q=p*p (linear) -> maybe pad cyclic length N=2n-1? Cyclic conv tensor. Compute Q = rfft(q, N?) If N=2n-1, cyclic conv p*p = linear plus wrap? Since linear degree 2n-2, no wrap if N≥2n-1. Actually cyclic conv length N=2n-1 of p with p equals linear (no wrap) because degree <N. Good.
- Want p_new such that p_new*p_new≈target q_target (linear). In cyclic length N=2n-1 (odd), compute Q_target = rfft(q_target). Want P_new = sqrt(Q_target) (complex). IDFT P (real?) Since target q real? Q may complex; sqrt branches. We can choose principal sqrt; idft may be complex; take real part and nonnegative? Because true nonnegative p if target is a square. If target q close to square, real part positive. This could produce a high-quality p. But taking sqrt of DFT with phase may produce alternating signs due branch cuts; not guaranteed nonnegative. We can project positive. Maybe clamp and normalize.

If q_target is flat 1 over N, Q[0]=1, others~0; sqrt gives uniform p, not sqrt flat? Wait flat q length 2n-1 cyclic: q uniform 1/N? DFT Q0=1, others 0; sqrt -> P0=1, IDFT uniform over N, but p length n? We pad p length n to N; IDFT uniform over all N, not support n. So not good. Need support n. The support constraint complicates.

Could use linear polynomial square root via formal series, not DFT.

Maybe use `scipy.signal.convolve`? no.

Another approach: Since p nonnegative and length n, objective is max of P(z)^2 coefficients. Use the fact p can be represented as |A(z)|^2? If P(z)=B(z) C(z) with ...? Nonnegative coefficients not guaranteed. If we allow complex, minimizing max convolution can use random Phase polynomials (Littlewood). But nonnegative constraint.

Could we exploit by letting sequence be squared of another with symmetric? Suppose p = c*c (convolution of c with itself) nonnegative, then p*p = c^4. Coefficients maybe flatter? If c is uniform over short, p binomial, p^2 coefficients? Not likely.

Maybe use nonnegative p as a probability distribution of a random walk with many steps; if c is ±1 random phase, p=|c|^2? Not length. For complex c with unit magnitude, autocorrelation c*c* can be low, but p=|c|? no.

Let's not overcomplicate.

    q = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(a,N)*np.fft.rfft(a,N), N) # can't use same? Need store
```

if q.size:

`reversed_weighted`:
```
def reversed_weighted(f, w, N, RDF):
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(W*RDF,N)
    n=len(f)
    out=c[n-1:n+n-1]
    if out.size:
        if np.min(out)<0: out=np.maximum(out,0.0)
    return out
```
Need `RDF` matches f. If f changes, recompute.

`resize_sequence`:
```
def resize_sequence(a,m):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a.size==m: return a
    if a.size==1: return np.full(m,1.0/m)
    # If downsample by averaging if m divides? Use interpolation.
    old=np.arange(a.size)
    # Map from [0,1]
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,m)
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size)
    out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    return normalize_nonneg(out)
```
`np.interp` requires x_old sorted; okay. It may produce tiny negative? no.

Maybe for upsample, linear interpolation of sequence values can reduce endpoint? okay.

`sqrt_flat_sequence`:
```
def sqrt_flat_sequence(n):
  p=np.empty(n)
  c=1.0
  p[0]=c
  for i in range(1,n):
     c *= (2*i -1)/(2.0*i)
     p[i]=c
  return normalize_nonneg(p)
```
This is O(n), for 32768 fine. Maybe start with this if parent missing.

`fourier_log_perturb`: Use `np.arange(n, dtype=float)`. K maybe int. For n large, K up to 64. amps: sigma / sqrt(k) or sigma/k? If sigma=0.1, K=64, total variance sum sigma^2/k ~0.011*4 =0.044 std. Good. Use `sigma * (1.0 / math.sqrt(k))`. Phases random. Could add a small random low-frequency. Return w centered.

`make_weights`: Use `v` as 1D. If max <=0: uniform. But h/q sum 1, max positive. If `M` very small, use uniform.
```
def make_weights(v,beta,blend=0.0):
    m=float(np.max(v)) if v.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-300:
       return np.full(v.size,1.0/v.size)
    x=(v-m)/m
    y=beta*x
    y=np.clip(y,-700.0,0.0)
    e=np.exp(y)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s<=0 or not finite:
       w=np.zeros(v.size); w[int(np.argmax(v))]=1; return w
    w=e/s
    if blend>0:
       tol=max(0.01*m, 1e-12)
       act = v >= m - tol
       na=int(act.sum())
       if na>0:
          w_act = act.astype(float)/na
          w = (1-blend)*w + blend*w_act
          s=w.sum(); if s>0: w/=s
    return w
```
blend tolerance 0.01 maybe top 2%. Could be too high; use 0.02.

In inner, if beta small, softmax nearly uniform; for h triangular, gradient smooth. Good.

`add_top_candidate`: top list can hold copies. For final, we need arrays. Use max_items 12.

def add_top_candidate(top,score,arr,max_items=12):
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return top
   cand=np.maximum(arr,0.0).copy()
   s=cand.sum()
   if s>0: cand/=s; score=... maybe not recompute? Use passed.
   # find worst
   worst=0; worst_score=inf
   for i in range(len(top)): if top[i][0] > worst_score...
   if len(top)<max_items or score < worst_score: replace/append
   return top
```
If we recompute normalize, score maybe invalid but close.

`exact_score_array`: Use direct. For n large and time? We'll just compute.
```
def exact_score_array(a):
   arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
   if arr.size==0: return inf
   arr=np.where(finite,arr,0); arr=np.maximum(arr,0)
   s=arr.sum()
   if s<0.01: scale...
   b=np.convolve(arr,arr)
   if b.size==0: return inf
   mx=float(b.max())
   if not finite: return inf
   val=2*arr.size*mx/(s*s)
   return val if finite else inf
```
If arr normalized sum1, val=2n max(b).

Could use `np.convolve(arr, arr, mode='full')` default full.

Pseudo-code more precise:
```
def optimize_sequence(a_start, target_n, seed, budget, verbose=True):
    start = time.monotonic()
    if budget <=0: return resize_sequence(a_start,target_n), resize_sequence..., fft_score, []
    deadline = start + max(1.0, budget - 3.0)
    f = resize_sequence(a_start, target_n)
    n=f.size
    N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    L=2*n-1
    phase=...
    A,q,f_score=fft_score_A_c(f,N,L)
    if not finite f_score:
       f=sqrt_flat_sequence(n); A,q,f_score=...
    best_f=f.copy(); best_score=f_score; top=add_top_candidate([],f_score,best_f)
    log_f=log_weights_from_sequence(f)
    log_b=log_f.copy(); b=f.copy()
    F=np.fft.rfft(f,N); RDF=phase*np.conj(F)
    m_b=np.zeros(n); v_b=np.zeros(n); t_b=0
    m_f=np.zeros(n); v_f=np.zeros(n); t_f=0
    beta_h=6.0; beta_s=10.0
    lr_h=0.10; lr_s=0.06
    stall=0; outer=0; last_self=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # if f_score close to 2, perturb
    if f_score > 1.99 and n>1:
       best_pert=f.copy(); best_pert_score=f_score
       for _ in range(12):
          sig=...
          w_try=log_weights(f)+... ; cand=...; sc=fft_score...
       f=...; recompute A,F,RDF; update best
    print(...)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       outer +=1
       # if best improvement maybe print
       if verbose and outer%100==0: print(outer, f_score, best_score)
       # sanity
       if not finite: reset
       # inner
       inner_steps = 6
       if outer % 4 == 0: inner_steps +=2
       if stall > 10: inner_steps += min(30, 2*stall)
       inner_steps = min(inner_steps, 50)
       for _ in range(inner_steps):
          if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
          B=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
          h=np.fft.irfft(F*B,N)
          h=h[:L]
          if h.size: 
             if np.min(h)<0: h=np.maximum(h,0)
             H=float(np.max(h))
          else H=0
          if not finite H: break
          blend_h = 0.0
          if beta_h > 30: blend_h=0.15
          w=make_weights(h,beta_h,blend_h)
          W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
          g=np.fft.irfft(W*RDF,N)
          seg=g[n-1:n+n-1]
          if seg.size:
             if np.min(seg)<0: seg=np.maximum(seg,0)
          # grad
          mean_g=float(np.dot(b,seg)) if b.size else 0
          grad_log=b*(seg-mean_g)
          if not all finite: break
          maxgrad=float(np.max(np.abs(grad_log)))
          if maxgrad < 1e-16: break
          log_b,t_b=adam_inplace(log_b,grad_log,m_b,v_b,t_b,lr_h,max_step=0.5)
          b=sequence_from_log_weights(log_b)
          if not all finite b: break
       # line search update f
       updated=False
       if all finite:
          best_t=0.0; best_cand_score=f_score; best_cand=None
          # maybe estimate H? no
          # order adaptive: if stall high try smaller? Use (0.5, 0.2, 1.0, 0.8, 0.35, 0.65)
          for tval in (0.5,0.2,1.0,0.8,0.35,0.65,0.1):
             cand=f*(1-tval)+b*tval
             cs=cand.sum()
             if cs<=0 or not finite cs: continue
             cand=cand/cs
             sc=fft_score_array(cand,N)
             if finite sc and sc < best_cand_score - 1e-15:
                best_cand_score=sc; best_cand=cand; best_t=tval
          if best_cand is not None:
             f=best_cand; f_score=best_cand_score
             log_f=log_weights_from_sequence(f)
             m_f=np.zeros(n); v_f=np.zeros(n); t_f=0
             F=np.fft.rfft(f,N); RDF=phase*np.conj(F)
             if best_t >= 0.99:
                b=f.copy(); log_b=log_weights_from_sequence(b); m_b=zeros...; t_b=0
             # else keep b state
             updated=True
             stall=0
             lr_h=0.10 if lr_h<0.10? maybe increase gradually: lr_h=min(0.20, lr_h*1.02)
             if f_score < best_score:
                best_f=f.copy(); best_score=f_score; top=add_top_candidate(top,best_score,best_f)
       else:
          stall += 1
          # reset b to f maybe
          if stall>3:
             b=f.copy(); log_b=log_weights(b); zeros state
       if not updated:
          stall += 1
          lr_h=max(0.03, lr_h*0.97)
          if stall>6:
             # pull b toward f
             log_b = 0.8*log_b + 0.2*log_f
             log_b -= max(log_b); b=softmax(log_b); m_b=zeros... t_b=0
       # beta schedule
       if outer % 30 ==0:
          beta_h = min(80.0, beta_h*1.04+0.15)
          beta_s = min(180.0, beta_s*1.04+0.15)
          lr_h = min(0.18, max(0.03, 0.12/(1+beta_h/40.0)))
          lr_s = min(0.10, max(0.02, 0.08/(1+beta_s/80.0)))
       # self step
       if (outer - last_self >= 20 or stall>30) and time.monotonic() < deadline:
          last_self=outer
          A,q,sq=fft_score_A_c(f,N,L)
          if finite sq and sq < f_score:
             f_score=sq; if f_score<best update; # maybe update F? f unchanged, A/RDF okay
          # attempt self steps
          improved_self=False
          for __ in range(4):
             blend_s=0.15 if beta_s>60 else 0.0
             w=make_weights(q,beta_s,blend_s)
             W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
             g=np.fft.irfft(W*RDF,N)
             seg=g[n-1:n+n-1]; max to 0
             grad_vec=2*seg
             mean_g=float(np.dot(f,grad_vec))
             grad_log=f*(grad_vec-mean_g)
             maxgrad=...
             if maxgrad<1e-15: break
             log_f_try,m_f_try,v_f_try,t_f_try=adam_candidate(log_f,grad_log,m_f,v_f,t_f,lr_s)
             cand=sequence_from_log_weights(log_f_try)
             sc=fft_score_array(cand,N)
             if finite sc and sc < f_score - 1e-15:
                f=cand; f_score=sc; log_f=log_f_try; m_f=m_f_try; v_f=v_f_try; t_f=t_f_try
                F=rfft(f); RDF=phase*conj(F)
                # reset inner b to f
                b=f.copy(); log_b=log_weights(b); m_b=zeros... t_b=0
                A,q,sq=fft_score_A_c(f,N,L)
                if f_score<best update
                improved_self=True
                break
             else:
                lr_s=max(0.01, lr_s*0.7)
          if improved_self:
              stall=0
               if lr_s<0.10: lr_s=min(0.10, lr_s*1.05)
               A,q,sq=fft_score_A_c(f,N,L)
          else:
               stall += 2
       # restart
       if stall > 70:
          if f_score > best_score + 0.02 or rng.random()<0.5:
             f=best_f.copy(); f_score=best_score
          else:
             K=int(max(4, min(64, n//128)))
             sig=float(rng.choice((0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4)))
             w_try=fourier_log_perturb(best_f,K,sig,rng)
             cand=sequence_from_log_weights(w_try)
             sc=fft_score_array(cand,N)
             if not finite sc or sc > best_score + 0.05:
                f=best_f.copy(); f_score=best_score
             else:
                f=cand; f_score=sc
          log_f=log_weights(f); m_f=zeros...
          b=f.copy(); log_b=log_weights(b); m_b=zeros...
          F=rfft(f); RDF=phase*conj(F)
          stall=0; lr_h=0.10; lr_s=0.06
          # beta maybe keep high? Reset to mid beta if new sequence
          beta_h=max(6.0, min(beta_h,20.0)); beta_s=max(10.0,min(beta_s,40.0))
    # final inside return: ensure best_f finite. If f better than best? Use best.
    return best_f, best_score, top
```
Need be careful: In self step, we use `A,q,sq`; after f update in self, F/RDF updated; but A variable not used further. Good.

`fft_score_A_c` returns A, q, score. If f updated in cross, we update F/RDF but not A/q. Self recompute.

Potential issue: `RDF=phase*conj(F)` needs F for f. If f update cross and best_t<1, RDF updated. b still from old f? F for inner new f updated, so inner uses new f. Good.

`phase` shape N//2+1. `np.conj(F)` same. fine.

When we do `log_b = 0.8*log_b + 0.2*log_f`, both uncentered maybe; but softmax invariant. Then subtract max. Good.

In `adam_inplace`, max_step 0.5 or 1.0? Large steps can cause b collapse. Set 1.0 maybe. If lr 0.1 and dw clipped 1 => step 0.1 log. okay. max_step=2.0? Use 1.0.

For self candidate, Adam may have v zeros first steps, dw large; clipped. Good.

Potential bug: `np.dot(b, seg)` if b and seg shapes? both length n. Good.

All arrays finite? We check but if h small negative clipped. If `np.max(h)` nan? handle.

Now, stage allocator in propose:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=2400,**kwargs):
   total=min(float(budget_s),2395.0)
   t0=time.monotonic()
   parent=load_initial_sequence()
   if parent is None:
      parent=sqrt_flat_sequence(4096)
   # Ensure parent not huge for resize; if >100k, downsample to 8192.
   if parent.size > 8192:
      parent=resize_sequence(parent,8192)
   candidates=[]
   # define stages: (n, alloc)
   stages=[(4096,0.08),(8192,0.22),(16384,0.30),(32768,0.30)]
   current_start=parent
   for n,alloc in stages:
      bud=total*alloc
      if bud < 5: continue
      print stage
      best_arr, best_score, top = optimize_sequence(current_start,n,int(seed) + n, bud, verbose=True)
      candidates.append((best_score,best_arr))
      # also add top? maybe global top list later; top arrays already in top; add best only for exact? Add all top to candidates if len manageable.
      for sc,a in top: candidates.append((sc,a))
      current_start = best_arr
      # maybe if time not enough? continue
   # exact validate
   final_best_seq=None; final_score=inf
   # sort candidates by score, but avoid exact too many. We'll validate unique by id? Use tuple of n and first few values? no.
   candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[0]) # lambda not allowed! Need no lambda. Use sort with key? The rule says no lambda functions. We can sort list of tuples without key because first element score. Good: candidates.sort().
   exact_limit = total*0.10
   exact_start=time.monotonic(); exact_time=0
   validated=set() # id of array? id stable while arrays exist. Could use id.
   for score,a in candidates:
       if time.monotonic()+exact_start - exact_start > exact_limit: break
       if len(a)>40000: continue? but 32768 ok.
       n=len(a)
       # estimate remaining time conservative
       remaining = (t0+total) - time.monotonic()
       if n>20000 and remaining < 60: continue
       if n>10000 and remaining < 30: continue
       # maybe cap number exact
       t_start=time.monotonic()
       ex=exact_score_array(a)
       exact_time+=time.monotonic()-t_start
       if finite ex:
           print exact score

Let's design a simple low-dimensional global optimizer:

Objective score = fft_score(p,N). Use gradient w.r.t theta: we can derive Jacobian of p. But we can use finite differences? d~50, gradient via finite differences would be 50 evaluations each too slow. Could use automatic chain: For given p and grad_log (as in self), objective w.r.t w = f*(grad - mean) (call grad_w). Then w.r.t theta_j = dot(grad_w, ∂w/∂theta_j). Jacobian ∂w/∂theta are Fourier basis values. We can compute for each theta by dot with vector of cos/sin. This is O(d n) per gradient, d=64, n=4096 =>262k ops, fine. Then use L-BFGS or our Adam in theta! This is a different algorithm: optimize a low-dimensional Fourier representation of log p. It may find global smoother shapes and is cheap. ```

def optimize_fourier(base, n, K, seed, budget, verbose=True):
   if n<64: return base resized?
   N=next_fast_len(2*n-1); L=2*n-1; phase=...
   d=2*K
   # Build B
   x=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
   B=np.empty((d,n), dtype=np.float64)
   for m in range(1,K+1):
      ang=2*np.pi*m*x/float(n)
      b=2*m-2
      np.cos(ang, out=B[b])
      np.sin(ang, out=B[b+1])
   norms = np.sum(B*B, axis=1)
   small norms set 1
   if base is not None:
      base_n=resize_sequence(base,n)
      w0=log_weights_from_sequence(base_n)
      w0c=w0-mean(w0)
      theta0=(B.T @ w0c)/norms
   else: theta0=zeros
   best_p = ... maybe base_n or uniform; best_score=fft_score(base_n,N)
   # if theta0 projection score maybe.
   for restart in limited time:
      theta = theta0.copy() + rng.normal(0,sig,d)
      m_th=zeros; v=zeros; t=0; lr=0.15; beta=10; stall=0
      p = softmax(theta@B); F,q,sc=...
      while time<deadline:
         # if no improvement etc
         w_soft=make_weights(q,beta)
         W=rfft(w_soft,N); h=irfft(W*RDF)[n-1:n+n-1]; max seg; grad_log=p*(2seg - 2dot(p,seg))
         th_grad=B.T @ grad_log
         th_try,...=adam_candidate(theta,th_grad,m,v,t,lr)
         p_try=softmax(th_try@B)
         sc_try=fft_score_array(p_try,N)
         if finite and sc_try < sc - 1e-13:
             accept; if sc_try<best: update best
             stall=0; lr=min(0.25, lr*1.02)
         else:
             lr=max(0.02, lr*0.85); stall+=1
         if stall>250: break restart
         # beta schedule
      ...
   return best_p

def finalize_sequence(a):
  arr=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
  arr=where finite positive

     arr=np.ones(n); s=n

  arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
  return [float(x) for x in arr]

Let's write functions concretely.

```
BETA1=0.9
BETA2=0.999

def adam_inplace(w, grad, m, v, t, lr, max_step=1.0):
    t += 1
    np.multiply(m, BETA1, out=m); m += (1-BETA1)*grad
    sq = grad*grad
    np.multiply(v, BETA2, out=v); v += (1-BETA2)*sq
    mhat = m/(1.0 - BETA1**t)
    vhat = v/(1.0 - BETA2**t)
    dw = mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-12)
    dw = np.where(np.isfinite(dw), dw, 0.0)
    dw = np.clip(dw, -max_step, max_step)
    w = w - lr*dw
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    mx=float(np.max(w))
    if np.isfinite(mx): w -= mx
    return w,t
```
Using `m += ...` may allocate? okay. Could do in-place safe.

`adam_candidate`:
```
def adam_candidate(w, grad, m, v, t, lr, max_step=1.0):
   tnew=t+1
   mnew = m*BETA1 + (1-BETA1)*grad
   vnew = v*BETA2 + (1-BETA2)*(grad*grad)
   mhat = mnew/(1-BETA1**tnew)
   vhat = vnew/(1-BETA2**tnew)
   dw=...
   wnew=...
   return wnew,mnew,vnew,tnew
```
If grad all zero, vnew zero, division by sqrt -> 0.

Implement:
```
def fft_score_A_c(a, N, L=None):
    n=len(a)
    if L is None: L=2*n-1
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    q=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
    if q.size > L: q=q[:L]
    elif q.size < L:
       # pad? should not
    if q.size:
       mn=float(np.min(q))
       if mn < 0: q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
       mx=float(np.max(q))
    else: mx=0
    if not finite: mx=0 score=inf
    return A,q, 2*n*mx
```
If n=0? not.

`fft_score_array` uses A,q but discards A. It may be slightly slower due creating q; okay.

If budget small, K maybe small.

Projection: If base is not None and base.size>0. `w0=log_weights_from_sequence(resize_sequence(base,n))`; `w0c=w0-np.mean(w0)`; `theta=(B.T @ w0c)/norms`.

For random, if base None, theta random.

Now, `I` not.

Potential performance issue in `optimize_fourier` `w = theta @ B` uses BLAS but B is C-order shape (d,n), theta (d,), result (n,). Good. Could use `np.dot(theta, B)`.

`th_grad = B.T @ grad_log`: B.T shape (n,d) non-contig, matmul maybe uses BLAS. okay.

Now, exact final: If exact_score_array returns inf for large due time? It doesn't time. We check before.

Use `total_budget = min(float(budget_s), 2395.0)`. If budget_s not provided? propose_candidate signature takes budget_s maybe. We'll have **kwargs.

Now, final code.

Let's ensure no lambda functions anywhere. Avoid `key=`.

Print statements: include moderate.

Now, final.
</think>

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative sequences minimizing

    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)**2)

under sum(a) = 1.  The program combines:
  - a Fourier low-resolution global polish,
  - an alternating min-max convex-subproblem / smoothed-max descent,
  - direct smoothed self-convolution gradient descent,
  - multi-resolution refinement from 4096/8192 to 32768,
  - throttled exact validation with numpy.convolve at the end.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np

BETA1 = 0.9
BETA2 = 0.999
EPS_LOG = 1e-300


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic utilities
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def next_fast_len(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def normalize_nonneg(v):
    """Return a finite nonnegative vector normalized to sum 1."""
    arr = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def log_weights_from_sequence(a):
    """Log of a nonnegative vector, centered for stability."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, EPS_LOG)
    w = np.log(a)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w = w - float(np.max(w))
    return w


def sequence_from_log_weights(w):
    """Stable softmax to a probability vector."""
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if w.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(w))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        mx = 0.0
    w = w - mx
    e = np.exp(w)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        e = np.exp(np.zeros_like(w))
        s = float(e.sum())
    return e / s


def resize_sequence(a, m):
    """Resize a sequence by linear interpolation on [0, 1]."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        m = 1
    if a.size == m:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_nonneg(out)


def sqrt_flat_sequence(n):
    """First n coefficients of (1 - z)^(-1/2), normalized."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c = 1.0
    p[0] = c
    for i in range(1, n):
        c *= (2 * i - 1) / (2.0 * i)
        p[i] = c
    return normalize_nonneg(p)


def make_weights(v, beta, blend=0.0):
    """
    Soft-max over shifted values v, optionally blended with uniform active tail.
    """
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(v))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(v.size, 1.0 / float(v.size), dtype=np.float64)

    x = (v - m) / m
    y = float(beta) * x
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, -700.0)
    y = np.clip(y, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(v.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(v))] = 1.0
        return w
    w = e / s

    if blend > 0.0:
        tol = max(0.02 * m, 1e-12)
        act = (v >= (m - tol))
        na = int(act.sum())
        if na > 0:
            w_act = act.astype(np.float64) / float(na)
            w = (1.0 - blend) * w + blend * w_act
            s2 = float(w.sum())
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 > 0.0:
                w = w / s2
    return w


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# FFT convolutions / gradients
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def fft_score_A_c(a, N, L=None):
    """
    FFT linear self-convolution of a, its DFT, and score 2*n*max(q).
    Assumes sum(a) = 1.
    """
    n = len(a)
    if L is None:
        L = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    q = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    if q.size > L:
        q = q[:L].copy()
    else:
        q = q.copy()
    if q.size:
        mn = float(np.min(q))
        if mn < 0.0:
            q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
        mx = float(np.max(q))
    else:
        mx = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return A, q, float("inf")
    return A, q, float(2.0 * n * mx)


def fft_score_array(a, N):
    """FFT score for normalized a."""
    _, _, sc = fft_score_A_c(a, N)
    return sc


def reversed_weighted(f, w, N, RDF):
    """
    Compute h_i = sum_j w_{i+j} f_j for i=0..n-1 from the DFT product
    W * RDF, where RDF is DFT of reversed f.
    """
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(W * RDF, N)
    n = len(f)
    out = c[n - 1:n + n - 1]
    out = out.copy()
    if out.size:
        mn = float(np.min(out))
        if mn < 0.0:
            out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Adam in log-space (inplace and candidate)
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def adam_inplace(w, grad, m, v, t, lr, max_step=1.0):
    """
    Adam step for log-weights in place.  m and v are modified.
    Returns new w and t.
    """
    t += 1
    m *= BETA1
    m += (1.0 - BETA1) * grad

    sq = grad * grad
    v *= BETA2
    v += (1.0 - BETA2) * sq

    mhat = m / (1.0 - BETA1 ** t)
    vhat = v / (1.0 - BETA2 ** t)
    dw = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-12)
    dw = np.where(np.isfinite(dw), dw, 0.0)
    dw = np.clip(dw, -float(max_step), float(max_step))

    w = w - float(lr) * dw
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(w))
    if np.isfinite(mx):
        w = w - mx
    return w, t


def adam_candidate(w, grad, m, v, t, lr, max_step=1.0):
    """
    Adam candidate step without modifying m,v,w.
    """
    tnew = t + 1
    mnew = m * BETA1 + (1.0 - BETA1) * grad
    sq = grad * grad
    vnew = v * BETA2 + (1.0 - BETA2) * sq

    mhat = mnew / (1.0 - BETA1 ** tnew)
    vhat = vnew / (1.0 - BETA2 ** tnew)
    dw = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-12)
    dw = np.where(np.isfinite(dw), dw, 0.0)
    dw = np.clip(dw, -float(max_step), float(max_step))

    wnew = w - float(lr) * dw
    wnew = np.where(np.isfinite(wnew), wnew, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(wnew))
    if np.isfinite(mx):
        wnew = wnew - mx
    return wnew, mnew, vnew, tnew


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Exact score
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def exact_score_array(a):
    """Exact objective using numpy.convolve."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    n = arr.size
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
            arr = arr * (0.01 / s)
        else:
            arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    try:
        b = np.convolve(arr, arr)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    val = float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else float("inf")


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Candidate bookkeeping
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def add_top_candidate(top, score, arr, max_items=12):
    """Keep a small list of best FFT-score candidates."""
    if arr is None or not len(arr):
        return top
    a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return top
    a = a / s
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return top

    if len(top) == 0:
        top.append((float(score), 0, a.copy()))
        return top

    worst = 0
    worst_idx = 0
    for i, item in enumerate(top):
        if i == 0 or item[0] > worst:
            worst = item[0]
            worst_idx = i

    if len(top) < max_items or score < worst:
        novel_idx = len(top) + 1
        if len(top) < max_items:
            top.append((float(score), novel_idx, a.copy()))
        else:
            top[worst_idx] = (float(score), novel_idx, a.copy())
    return top


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Fourier projection restart for diversity
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def fourier_log_perturb(a, K, sigma, rng):
    """Add a smooth random Fourier perturbation to log weights."""
    w = log_weights_from_sequence(a)
    n = len(w)
    if n == 0 or K < 1:
        return w
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    delta = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, int(K) + 1):
        amp = float(sigma) / math.sqrt(float(k))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        delta += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x / float(n) + ph)
    w = w + delta
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w -= float(np.max(w))
    return w


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Fourier-parameterized global / smooth optimizer
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def optimize_fourier(base, n, K, seed, budget, verbose=True):
    """
    Optimize a low-dimensional Fourier representation of log p.
    Returns the best probability vector found.
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget) - 3.0)
    deadline = start + budget
    n = int(n)
    if n < 64:
        if base is None:
            return sqrt_flat_sequence(max(n, 1))
        return resize_sequence(base, n)

    K = max(4, min(int(K), max(4, n // 32)))
    d = 2 * K
    N = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    phase = np.exp(-2j * np.pi * np.arange(N // 2 + 1) * (n - 1) / float(N))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    B = np.empty((d, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, K + 1):
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * m * x / float(n)
        row = 2 * m - 2
        B[row, :] = np.cos(ang)
        B[row + 1, :] = np.sin(ang)
    norms = np.sum(B * B, axis=1)
    norms = np.where(np.isfinite(norms) & (norms > 1e-12), norms, 1.0)

    base_n = None
    if base is not None and len(base) > 0:
        base_n = resize_sequence(base, n)
    else:
        base_n = sqrt_flat_sequence(n)

    best_p = base_n.copy()
    best_score = fft_score_array(best_p, N)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = float("inf")

    theta0 = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
    w0 = log_weights_from_sequence(base_n)
    w0c = w0 - float(np.mean(w0))
    theta0 = (B.T @ w0c) / norms
    theta0 = np.where(np.isfinite(theta0), theta0, 0.0)

    if verbose:
        print("FOURIER n=%d K=%d budget=%.1f base=%.6f" %
              (n, K, budget, best_score), flush=True)

    restart_count = 0
    last_best = start

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # Initialize a restart.
        sig = float(min(0.45, 0.05 * (1.0 + 0.18 * restart_count)))
        theta = theta0.copy()
        theta += rng.normal(0.0, sig, size=d)
        theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)

        m_th = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
        v_th = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
        t_th = 0
        lr = 0.12
        beta = 12.0
        stall = 0
        outer = 0
        p = sequence_from_log_weights(theta @ B)

        restart_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(30.0, budget / 8.0))

        while time.monotonic() < restart_deadline:
            outer += 1
            F, q, sc = fft_score_A_c(p, N, L)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                break

            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_p = p.copy()
                last_best = time.monotonic()

            RDF = phase * np.conj(F)
            blend_s = 0.
            if beta > 60.0:
                blend_s = 0.15
            w_soft = make_weights(q, beta, blend_s)
            W = np.fft.rfft(w_soft, N)
            h = np.fft.irfft(W * RDF, N)
            seg = h[n - 1:n + n - 1].copy()
            if seg.size:
                mn = float(np.min(seg))
                if mn < 0.0:
                    seg = np.maximum(seg, 0.0)
            grad_vec = 2.0 * seg
            mean_g = float(np.dot(p, grad_vec))
            grad_log = p * (grad_vec - mean_g)
            maxgrad = float(np.max(np.abs(grad_log))) if grad_log.size else 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(maxgrad) or maxgrad < 1e-16:
                stall += 20
            else:
                th_grad = B.T @ grad_log
                th_grad = np.where(np.isfinite(th_grad), th_grad, 0.0)
                th_try, m_try, v_try, t_try = adam_candidate(theta, th_grad, m_th, v_th, t_th, lr, 1.5)
                p_try = sequence_from_log_weights(th_try @ B)
                sc_try = fft_score_array(p_try, N)

                if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < sc - 1e-14:
                    theta = th_try
                    m_th = m_try
                    v_th = v_try
                    t_th = t_try
                    p = p_try
                    stale_best = False
                else:
                    lr = max(0.02, lr * 0.82)
                    stale_best = True

            # schedule
            if outer % 40 == 0:
                beta = min(180.0, beta * 1.05 + 0.2)
                lr = min(0.25, max(0.02, 0.15 / (1.0 + beta / 80.0)))
            if stale_best:
                stall += 1

            if stall > 220 or (time.monotonic() - last_best > max(45.0, budget / 6.0) and best_score < np.inf and sc > best_score * 1.01):
                break

        restart_count += 1
        if verbose and (restart_count % 3 == 0):
            print("FOURIER restart=%d best=%.6f time=%.1f" %
                  (restart_count, best_score, time.monotonic() - start), flush=True)

    if verbose:
        print("FOURIER done best=%.6f" % best_score, flush=True)
    return best_p


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main high-resolution optimizer
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def optimize_sequence(a_start, target_n, seed, budget, verbose=True):
    """
    Main hybrid optimizer for one target length.
    Returns (best_normalized_array, best_fft_score, top_list)
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget) - 3.0)
    deadline = start + budget
    n = int(target_n)

    f = resize_sequence(a_start, n)
    N = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    phase = np.exp(-2j * np.pi * np.arange(N // 2 + 1) * (n - 1) / float(N))

    A, q, f_score = fft_score_A_c(f, N, L)
    if not np.isfinite(f_score):
        f = sqrt_flat_sequence(n)
        A, q, f_score = fft_score_A_c(f, N, L)

    best_f = f.copy()
    best_score = f_score
    top = add_top_candidate([], best_score, best_f)

    log_f = log_weights_from_sequence(f)
    log_b = log_f.copy()
    b = f.copy()

    F = np.fft.rfft(f, N)
    RDF = phase * np.conj(F)

    m_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t_b = 0

    m_f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v_f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t_f = 0

    beta_h = 6.0
    beta_s = 10.0
    lr_h = 0.10
    lr_s = 0.06

    stall = 0
    outer = 0
    last_self = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    if verbose:
        print("OPT n=%d start_score=%.6f" % (n, f_score), flush=True)

    # If extremely close to uniform, break symmetry immediately.
    if f_score > 1.99 and n > 1:
        best_pert = f.copy()
        best_pert_score = f_score
        for _ in range(16):
            sig = float(rng.choice((0.03, 0.07, 0.15, 0.35, 0.8)))
            w_try = log_weights_from_sequence(f) + rng.normal(0.0, sig, size=n)
            cand = sequence_from_log_weights(w_try)
            sc = fft_score_array(cand, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_pert_score:
                best_pert_score = sc
                best_pert = cand.copy()
        f = best_pert.copy()
        f_score = best_pert_score
        log_f = log_weights_from_sequence(f)
        F = np.fft.rfft(f, N)
        RDF = phase * np.conj(F)
        if f_score < best_score:
            best_score = f_score
            best_f = f.copy()
            top = add_top_candidate(top, best_score, best_f)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        outer += 1

        # Safety reset.
        if (not np.isfinite(f_score) or
                not np.all(np.isfinite(f)) or
                not np.all(np.isfinite(b)) or
                not np.all(np.isfinite(log_b))):
            f = best_f.copy()
            f_score = best_score
            log_f = log_weights_from_sequence(f)
            m_f.fill(0.0); v_f.fill(0.0); t_f = 0
            b = f.copy()
            log_b = log_weights_from_sequence(b)
            m_b.fill(0.0); v_b.fill(0.0); t_b = 0
            F = np.fft.rfft(f, N)
            RDF = phase * np.conj(F)
            stall = 0
            continue

        if verbose and outer % 150 == 0:
            print("OPT n=%d outer=%d cur=%.6f best=%.6f" %
                  (n, outer, f_score, best_score), flush=True)

        # -------------------- inner convex subproblem --------------------
        inner_steps = 12
        if outer % 3 == 0:
            inner_steps += 4
        if stall > 10:
            inner_steps += min(50, 3 * stall)
        inner_steps = min(inner_steps, 100)

        H = 0.0
        for _ in range(inner_steps):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            Bx = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
            h = np.fft.irfft(F * Bx, N)
            h = h[:L]
            if h.size:
                if float(np.min(h)) < 0.0:
                    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
                H = float(np.max(h))
            else:
                H = 0.0

            if not np.isfinite(H):
                break

            blend_h = 0.0
            if beta_h > 30.0:
                blend_h = 0.15
            w_soft = make_weights(h, beta_h, blend_h)
            W = np.fft.rfft(w_soft, N)
            g = np.fft.irfft(W * RDF, N)
            seg = g[n - 1:n + n - 1].copy()
            if seg.size:
                if float(np.min(seg)) < 0.0:
                    seg = np.maximum(seg, 0.0)

            mean_g = float(np.dot(b, seg))
            grad_log = b * (seg - mean_g)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_log)):
                break
            maxgrad = float(np.max(np.abs(grad_log))) if grad_log.size else 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(maxgrad) or maxgrad < 1e-16:
                break

            log_b, t_b = adam_inplace(log_b, grad_log, m_b, v_b, t_b, lr_h, 1.0)
            b = sequence_from_log_weights(log_b)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
                break

        # -------------------- line search f <- (1-t) f + t b --------------------
        updated = False
        if np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
            best_t = 0.0
            best_cand_score = f_score
            best_cand = None

            candidates = (0.5, 1.0, 0.2, 0.8, 0.35)
            for tval in candidates:
                cand = (1.0 - tval) * f + tval * b
                cs = float(cand.sum())
                if not np.isfinite(cs) or cs <= 0.0:
                    continue
                cand = cand / cs
                sc = fft_score_array(cand, N)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_cand_score - 1e-15:
                    best_cand_score = sc
                    best_cand = cand
                    best_t = tval

            if best_cand is not None:
                f = best_cand
                f_score = best_cand_score
                log_f = log_weights_from_sequence(f)
                m_f.fill(0.0); v_f.fill(0.0); t_f = 0
                F = np.fft.rfft(f, N)
                RDF = phase * np.conj(F)

                if best_t >= 0.99:
                    b = f.copy()
                    log_b = log_weights_from_sequence(b)
                    m_b.fill(0.0); v_b.fill(0.0); t_b = 0

                updated = True
                stall = 0
                lr_h = min(0.22, lr_h * 1.02)

                if f_score < best_score:
                    best_score = f_score
                    best_f = f.copy()
                    top = add_top_candidate(top, best_score, best_f)
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 3:
                b = f.copy()
                log_b = log_weights_from_sequence(b)
                m_b.fill(0.0); v_b.fill(0.0); t_b = 0

        if not updated:
            stall += 1
            lr_h = max(0.03, lr_h * 0.97)
            if stall > 6:
                log_f_c = log_f - float(np.max(log_f))
                log_b_c = log_b - float(np.max(log_b))
                log_b = 0.8 * log_b_c + 0.2 * log_f_c
                log_b = np.where(np.isfinite(log_b), log_b, 0.0)
                log_b -= float(np.max(log_b))
                b = sequence_from_log_weights(log_b)
                m_b.fill(0.0); v_b.fill(0.0); t_b = 0

        # -------------------- beta schedule --------------------
        if outer % 30 == 0:
            beta_h = min(90.0, beta_h * 1.04 + 0.15)
            beta_s = min(200.0, beta_s * 1.04 + 0.15)
            lr_h = min(0.18, max(0.03, 0.13 / (1.0 + beta_h / 45.0)))
            lr_s = min(0.11, max(0.02, 0.08 / (1.0 + beta_s / 90.0)))

        # -------------------- periodic direct self-gradient --------------------
        if (outer - last_self >= 25 or stall > 35) and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            last_self = outer
            A, q, sq = fft_score_A_c(f, N, L)
            if np.isfinite(sq) and sq < f_score:
                f_score = sq
                if f_score < best_score:
                    best_score = f_score
                    best_f = f.copy()
                    top = add_top_candidate(top, best_score, best_f)

            improved_self = False
            F = np.fft.rfft(f, N)
            RDF = phase * np.conj(F)

            for _ in range(6):
                blend_s = 0.
                if beta_s > 60.0:
                    blend_s = 0.15
                w_soft = make_weights(q, beta_s, blend_s)
                W = np.fft.rfft(w_soft, N)
                g = np.fft.irfft(W * RDF, N)
                seg = g[n - 1:n + n - 1].copy()
                if seg.size:
                    if float(np.min(seg)) < 0.0:
                        seg = np.maximum(seg, 0.0)

                grad_vec = 2.0 * seg
                mean_g = float(np.dot(f, grad_vec))
                grad_log = f * (grad_vec - mean_g)
                maxgrad = float(np.max(np.abs(grad_log))) if grad_log.size else 0.0
                if not np.isfinite(maxgrad) or maxgrad < 1e-16:
                    break

                log_f_try, m_f_try, v_f_try, t_f_try = adam_candidate(
                    log_f, grad_log, m_f, v_f, t_f, lr_s, 1.0
                )
                cand = sequence_from_log_weights(log_f_try)
                sc = fft_score_array(cand, N)

                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < f_score - 1e-15:
                    f = cand
                    f_score = sc
                    log_f = log_f_try
                    m_f = m_f_try
                    v_f = v_f_try
                    t_f = t_f_try

                    F = np.fft.rfft(f, N)
                    RDF = phase * np.conj(F)

                    b = f.copy()
                    log_b = log_weights_from_sequence(b)
                    m_b.fill(0.0); v_b.fill(0.0); t_b = 0

                    A, q, sq = fft_score_A_c(f, N, L)
                    if f_score < best_score:
                        best_score = f_score
                        best_f = f.copy()
                        top = add_top_candidate(top, best_score, best_f)

                    improved_self = True
                    break
                else:
                    lr_s = max(0.01, lr_s * 0.72)

            if improved_self:
                stall = 0
                lr_s = min(0.12, lr_s * 1.05)
            else:
                stall += 3

        # -------------------- restart / exploration --------------------
        if stall > 80:
            if f_score > best_score + 0.02 or rng.random() < 0.55:
                f = best_f.copy()
                f_score = best_score
            else:
                Kp = int(max(4, min(64, n // 128)))
                sig = float(rng.choice((0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40)))
                w_try = fourier_log_perturb(best_f, Kp, sig, rng)
                cand = sequence_from_log_weights(w_try)
                sc = fft_score_array(cand, N)
                if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc > best_score + 0.04:
                    f = best_f.copy()
                    f_score = best_score
                else:
                    f = cand
                    f_score = sc

            log_f = log_weights_from_sequence(f)
            m_f.fill(0.0); v_f.fill(0.0); t_f = 0
            b = f.copy()
            log_b = log_weights_from_sequence(b)
            m_b.fill(0.0); v_b.fill(0.0); t_b = 0
            F = np.fft.rfft(f, N)
            RDF = phase * np.conj(F)
            stall = 0
            lr_h = 0.11
            lr_s = 0.07
            beta_h = min(beta_h, 25.0)
            beta_s = min(beta_s, 50.0)

    # Final local best may be better than recorded best if numerical drift.
    if np.isfinite(f_score) and f_score < best_score:
        best_score = f_score
        best_f = f.copy()
        top = add_top_candidate(top, best_score, best_f)

    if verbose:
        print("OPT n=%d done best_fft=%.*f" % (n, 9, best_score), flush=True)
    return best_f, best_score, top


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Initial sequence loading / start selection
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def load_initial_sequence():
    """Load provided SOTA sequence if available."""
    obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if obj is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size < 16:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    arr = arr / s
    if arr.size > 16384:
        arr = resize_sequence(arr, 8192)
    return arr


def pick_start(pool, target_n):
    """Pick the best initializing sequence for a target length."""
    target_n = int(target_n)
    N = next_fast_len(2 * target_n - 1)
    cands = []
    idx = 0
    for a in pool:
        if a is None or len(a) == 0:
            continue
        r = resize_sequence(a, target_n)
        sc = fft_score_array(r, N)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            cands.append((sc, idx, r))
        idx += 1

    best_score = float("inf")
    for sc, _, _ in cands:
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc

    # If everything is poor, add the robust sqrt-flat construction.
    if len(cands) == 0 or best_score > 1.9:
        r = sqrt_flat_sequence(target_n)
        sc = fft_score_array(r, N)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            cands.append((sc, idx, r))
            idx += 1

    if len(cands) == 0:
        return sqrt_flat_sequence(target_n)

    cands.sort()
    return cands[0][2]


def prune_pool(pool, target_n, keep=4):
    """Keep a small pool of best sequences by score at current target."""
    target_n = int(target_n)
    N = next_fast_len(2 * target_n - 1)
    scored = []
    idx = 0
    seen = set()
    for a in pool:
        if a is None or len(a) == 0:
            continue
        obj_id = id(a)
        if obj_id in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(obj_id)
        r = resize_sequence(a, target_n)
        sc = fft_score_array(r, N)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, idx, a.copy()))
        idx += 1
    if not scored:
        return [sqrt_flat_sequence(max(target_n, 64))]
    scored.sort()
    out = [a for _, _, a in scored[:int(keep)]]
    return out


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Finalization
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def finalize_sequence(a):
    """Return a valid Python list of nonnegative floats normalized to sum 1."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.size)
    arr = arr / s
    # Keep values safely within evaluator caps.
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s2 = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 < 0.01:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        arr = arr / s2
    return [float(x) for x in arr]


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Entry point
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Run multi-quality search and return the best found sequence.
    """
    total_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total = min(float(budget_s), 2395.0)
    except Exception:
        total = 2395.0
    total = max(60.0, total)
    seed = int(seed)

    print("PROPOSE total=%.1fs seed=%d" % (total, seed), flush=True)

    parent = load_initial_sequence()
    if parent is None:
        parent = sqrt_flat_sequence(4096)
        print("No parent sequence; using sqrt-flat 4096 initialization.", flush=True)
    else:
        print("Loaded parent length=%d" % len(parent), flush=True)

    pool = [parent, sqrt_flat_sequence(4096)]
    all_candidates = []

    # Quick low-dimensional Fourier global track.
    fourier_alloc = 0.08
    if total > 900.0:
        bud_f = total * fourier_alloc
        n_f = 8192
        K_f = int(max(16, min(96, n_f // 64)))
        start_f = pick_start(pool, n_f)
        fp = optimize_fourier(start_f, n_f, K_f, seed + 1000, bud_f, verbose=True)
        if fp is not None and len(fp) == n_f:
            Nf = next_fast_len(2 * n_f - 1)
            scf = fft_score_array(fp, Nf)
            all_candidates.append((scf if np.isfinite(scf) else float("inf"), 5000, fp.copy()))
            pool = prune_pool(pool + [fp], n_f, keep=5)
            print("FOURIER track added, pool prune done.", flush=True)

    # Multi-resolution optimization. Allocations sum to 0.82 for >=2400s.
    if total > 1400.0:
        stages = [
            (8192, 0.17),
            (16384, 0.28),
            (32768, 0.37),
        ]
    elif total > 700.0:
        stages = [
            (8192, 0.30),
            (16384, 0.40),
            (32768, 0.20),
        ]
    else:
        stages = [
            (8192, 0.45),
            (16384, 0.45),
        ]

    for stage_idx, (target_n, alloc) in enumerate(stages):
        if time.monotonic() - total_start > total * 0.92:
            break
        stage_budget = total * alloc
        if stage_budget < 12.0:
            continue
        print("STAGE %d n=%d budget=%.1f" % (stage_idx, target_n, stage_budget), flush=True)
        start = pick_start(pool, target_n)
        best_arr, best_score, top = optimize_sequence(
            start, target_n, seed + 100 + 29 * stage_idx, stage_budget, verbose=True
        )

        if best_arr is not None and len(best_arr) > 0:
            all_candidates.append((best_score if np.isfinite(best_score) else float("inf"),
                                   1000 + stage_idx * 10, best_arr.copy()))
            for sc_top, _, arr_top in top:
                if arr_top is not None and len(arr_top) > 0:
                    all_candidates.append((sc_top if np.isfinite(sc_top) else float("inf"),
                                           int(time.monotonic() * 1000) % 1000000, arr_top.copy()))
            pool = prune_pool(pool + [best_arr], target_n, keep=5)

    # Exact validation / final selection.
    final_seq = None
    final_score = float("inf")
    exact_limit = max(60.0, total * 0.09)
    exact_start = time.monotonic()
    exact_count = 0
    max_exact = 16
    seen_ids = set()

    # Sort candidates by FFT score, then candidate index; arrays not compared.
    all_candidates.sort()

    print("VALIDATING %d candidates by exact convolve..." % len(all_candidates), flush=True)

    for sc_fft, idx, arr in all_candidates:
        if exact_count >= max_exact:
            break
        if time.monotonic() - exact_start > exact_limit:
            break
        n = len(arr)
        if n <= 0:
            continue
        remaining = (total_start + total) - time.monotonic()
        if n > 25000 and remaining < 80.0:
            continue
        if n > 12000 and remaining < 40.0:
            continue
        if n > 6000 and remaining < 20.0:
            continue

        obj_id = id(arr)
        if obj_id in seen_ids:
            continue
        seen_ids.add(obj_id)

        arr_c = normalize_nonneg(arr)
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        ex = exact_score_array(arr_c)
        dt = time.monotonic() - t0
        exact_count += 1
        if np.isfinite(ex):
            print("EXACT n=%d fft=%.6f exact=%.6f time=%.2f" % (n, sc_fft, ex, dt), flush=True)
            if ex < final_score:
                final_score = ex
                final_seq = arr_c
        else:
            print("EXACT n=%d failed fft=%.6f time=%.2f" % (n, sc_fft, dt), flush=True)

        if final_score < 1.40:
            break

    # Fallback: use best by FFT if exact validation did not produce anything.
    if final_seq is None:
        if all_candidates:
            final_seq = normalize_nonneg(all_candidates[0][2])
        else:
            final_seq = normalize_nonneg(parent)

    print("FINAL selected exact known=%s score=%.*f len=%d remaining=%.1f" %
          ("yes" if np.isfinite(final_score) else "no", 9,
           final_score if np.isfinite(final_score) else -1,
           len(final_seq), (total_start + total) - time.monotonic()), flush=True)

    out = finalize_sequence(final_seq)
    return out
```
